declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t816 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/multiasync.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t818 = alloca i32
  %t817 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t816, ptr %t818, ptr null)
  %t819 = icmp ne i32 %t817, 0
  br i1 %t819, label %main.async.fail.86, label %main.async.ok.86

  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.86:
  %t820 = load i32, ptr %t818
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t820
}
